     1 +---
     2 +name: recent-download-analysis
     3 +description: Analyze the most recent files in Downloads, especially mixed batches of scree
        nshots, videos, and text docs. Use when the user wants first-pass summaries, extracted ide
        as, or catalog reports for recent downloads, and when Gemini multimodal analysis is useful
        .
     4 +---
     5 +
     6 +# Recent Download Analysis
     7 +
     8 +Use this skill when the user wants a bounded pass over the latest files in `Downloads` and
         needs structured summaries per file.
     9 +
    10 +## Workflow
    11 +
    12 +1. Run `scripts/analyze_recent_downloads.py`.
    13 +2. Point it at the target folder, item count, and output directory.
    14 +3. Let the script emit both Markdown and JSON artifacts.
    15 +4. Read the generated Markdown report, then save durable memory in the workspace:
    16 +   - add or update a report in `reports/`
    17 +   - add an IX entry for the batch or the most important findings
    18 +   - update `TASKS.md` if another pass is pending
    19 +
    20 +## Command
    21 +
    22 +```powershell
    23 +python C:\Users\acer\.codex\skills\recent-download-analysis\scripts\analyze_recent_downloa
        ds.py `
    24 +  --downloads-dir C:\Users\acer\Downloads `
    25 +  --count 15 `
    26 +  --output-dir C:\Users\acer\Asolaria\reports
    27 +```
    28 +
    29 +## What The Script Does
    30 +
    31 +- enumerates the most recent files
    32 +- uses Gemini for first-pass summaries
    33 +- sends images inline
    34 +- uploads videos through the Gemini File API and waits for processing
    35 +- summarizes text/markdown locally through Gemini text input
    36 +- writes:
    37 +  - a Markdown report
    38 +  - a machine-readable JSON file
    39 +
    40 +## Environment
    41 +
    42 +The script checks these env vars in order:
    43 +
    44 +- `GEMINI_API_KEY`
    45 +- `GOOGLE_API_KEY`
    46 +- `GOOGLE_DEVKNOWLEDGE_API_KEY`
    47 +
    48 +Never print or persist the raw key.
    49 +
    50 +## Output Discipline
    51 +
    52 +- First pass: concise summary, visible text, key ideas, and action signals for each file.
    53 +- Deep interpretation belongs in the next pass, after the first-pass report is stored and
        indexed.
  └ ~\.codex\skills\recent-download-analysis\scripts\analyze_recent_downloads.py (+422 -0)
      1 +#!/usr/bin/env python3
      2 +import argparse
      3 +import base64
      4 +import json
      5 +import mimetypes
      6 +import os
      7 +import re
      8 +import sys
      9 +import time
     10 +import urllib.error
     11 +import urllib.parse
     12 +import urllib.request
     13 +from datetime import datetime
     14 +from pathlib import Path
     15 +
     16 +from PIL import Image
     17 +
     18 +
     19 +IMAGE_EXTS = {".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp", ".gif", ".bmp"}
     20 +VIDEO_EXTS = {".mp4", ".mov", ".m4v", ".avi", ".webm", ".mkv"}
     21 +TEXT_EXTS = {".md", ".txt", ".json", ".csv", ".log", ".yaml", ".yml"}
     22 +
     23 +
     24 +def parse_args():
     25 +    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
     26 +        description="Analyze the most recent downloaded files with Gemini and emit Markdo
         wn + JSON reports."
     27 +    )
     28 +    parser.add_argument("--downloads-dir", default=str(Path.home() / "Downloads"))
     29 +    parser.add_argument("--count", type=int, default=15)
     30 +    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True)
     31 +    parser.add_argument("--model", default="gemini-2.5-flash")
     32 +    parser.add_argument("--prefix", default="recent-downloads-first-pass")
     33 +    parser.add_argument("--max-text-chars", type=int, default=20000)
     34 +    return parser.parse_args()
     35 +
     36 +
     37 +def pick_api_key():
     38 +    for name in ("GEMINI_API_KEY", "GOOGLE_API_KEY", "GOOGLE_DEVKNOWLEDGE_API_KEY"):
     39 +        value = os.environ.get(name)
     40 +        if value and value.strip():
     41 +            return value.strip(), name
     42 +    raise RuntimeError("Missing Gemini API key env var.")
     43 +
     44 +
     45 +def iso_now():
     46 +    return datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
     47 +
     48 +
     49 +def stamp_now():
     50 +    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
     51 +
     52 +
     53 +def http_json(method, url, headers=None, payload=None, timeout=120):
     54 +    body = None
     55 +    merged_headers = dict(headers or {})
     56 +    if payload is not None:
     57 +        body = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
     58 +        merged_headers.setdefault("Content-Type", "application/json")
     59 +    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, headers=merged_headers, method=method)
     60 +    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
     61 +        raw = resp.read()
     62 +        return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8")), dict(resp.headers.items())
     63 +
     64 +
     65 +def http_bytes(method, url, headers=None, data=None, timeout=300):
     66 +    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers=dict(headers or {}), method=meth
         od)
     67 +    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
     68 +        raw = resp.read()
     69 +        return raw, dict(resp.headers.items())
     70 +
     71 +
     72 +def extract_text_from_response(response):
     73 +    candidates = response.get("candidates") or []
     74 +    if not candidates:
     75 +        raise RuntimeError(f"No Gemini candidates in response: {response}")
     76 +    parts = candidates[0].get("content", {}).get("parts", [])
     77 +    text = "".join(part.get("text", "") for part in parts if isinstance(part, dict))
     78 +    return text.strip()
     79 +
     80 +
     81 +def strip_json_fence(text):
     82 +    cleaned = text.strip()
     83 +    cleaned = re.sub(r"^```json\s*", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
     84 +    cleaned = re.sub(r"^```\s*", "", cleaned)
     85 +    cleaned = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", cleaned)
     86 +    return cleaned.strip()
     87 +
     88 +
     89 +def parse_json_text(text):
     90 +    cleaned = strip_json_fence(text)
     91 +    return json.loads(cleaned)
     92 +
     93 +
     94 +def generation_prompt(file_name, file_kind):
     95 +    return (
     96 +        "You are doing first-pass cataloging for Jesse's recent downloads. "
     97 +        f"Analyze this {file_kind} named '{file_name}'. "
     98 +        "Return strict JSON with keys: "
     99 +        "summary (string), visible_text (array of strings), primary_subjects (array of st
         rings), "
    100 +        "key_ideas (array of strings), action_signals (array of strings), sensitivity_fla
         gs (array of strings), "
    101 +        "confidence (string: low|medium|high). "
    102 +        "Keep it concise, factual, and first-pass only. "
    103 +        "If something is uncertain, say so in the summary or lower confidence."
    104 +    )
    105 +
    106 +
    107 +def generate_content(api_key, model, parts):
    108 +    url = (
    109 +        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/"
    110 +        + urllib.parse.quote(model)
    111 +        + ":generateContent?key="
    112 +        + urllib.parse.quote(api_key)
    113 +    )
    114 +    payload = {
    115 +        "contents": [{"parts": parts}],
    116 +        "generationConfig": {"responseMimeType": "application/json"},
    117 +    }
    118 +    data, _ = http_json("POST", url, payload=payload)
    119 +    return parse_json_text(extract_text_from_response(data))
    120 +
    121 +
    122 +def upload_file(api_key, file_path, mime_type):
    123 +    start_url = (
    124 +        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/upload/v1beta/files?key="
    125 +        + urllib.parse.quote(api_key)
    126 +    )
    127 +    start_headers = {
    128 +        "X-Goog-Upload-Protocol": "resumable",
    129 +        "X-Goog-Upload-Command": "start",
    130 +        "X-Goog-Upload-Header-Content-Length": str(file_path.stat().st_size),
    131 +        "X-Goog-Upload-Header-Content-Type": mime_type,
    132 +        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    133 +    }
    134 +    start_payload = {"file": {"display_name": file_path.name}}
    135 +    _, resp_headers = http_json("POST", start_url, headers=start_headers, payload=start_p
         ayload, timeout=120)
    136 +    upload_url = resp_headers.get("X-Goog-Upload-URL") or resp_headers.get("x-goog-upload
         -url")
    137 +    if not upload_url:
    138 +        raise RuntimeError("Gemini upload start response missing X-Goog-Upload-URL.")
    139 +    data = file_path.read_bytes()
    140 +    upload_headers = {
    141 +        "Content-Length": str(len(data)),
    142 +        "X-Goog-Upload-Offset": "0",
    143 +        "X-Goog-Upload-Command": "upload, finalize",
    144 +    }
    145 +    raw, _ = http_bytes("POST", upload_url, headers=upload_headers, data=data, timeout=60
         0)
    146 +    parsed = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
    147 +    return parsed["file"]
    148 +
    149 +
    150 +def get_file(api_key, name):
    151 +    url = (
    152 +        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/"
    153 +        + urllib.parse.quote(name, safe="/")
    154 +        + "?key="
    155 +        + urllib.parse.quote(api_key)
    156 +    )
    157 +    data, _ = http_json("GET", url, timeout=120)
    158 +    return data
    159 +
    160 +
    161 +def delete_file(api_key, name):
    162 +    url = (
    163 +        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/"
    164 +        + urllib.parse.quote(name, safe="/")
    165 +        + "?key="
    166 +        + urllib.parse.quote(api_key)
    167 +    )
    168 +    try:
    169 +        http_json("DELETE", url, timeout=120)
    170 +    except Exception:
    171 +        pass
    172 +
    173 +
    174 +def wait_until_active(api_key, file_obj, max_wait_seconds=600):
    175 +    started = time.time()
    176 +    current = file_obj
    177 +    while True:
    178 +        state = ((current.get("state") or {}).get("name") or current.get("state") or "").
         upper()
    179 +        if state in {"ACTIVE", "SUCCEEDED"}:
    180 +            return current
    181 +        if state in {"FAILED", "ERROR"}:
    182 +            raise RuntimeError(f"Gemini file processing failed: {current}")
    183 +        if time.time() - started > max_wait_seconds:
    184 +            raise TimeoutError(f"Timed out waiting for Gemini file to become ACTIVE: {cur
         rent.get('name')}")
    185 +        time.sleep(5)
    186 +        current = get_file(api_key, current["name"])
    187 +
    188 +
    189 +def read_text_excerpt(path, max_chars):
    190 +    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    191 +    return text[:max_chars]
    192 +
    193 +
    194 +def image_metadata(path):
    195 +    try:
    196 +        with Image.open(path) as image:
    197 +            return {"width": image.width, "height": image.height}
    198 +    except Exception:
    199 +        return {}
    200 +
    201 +
    202 +def classify(path):
    203 +    ext = path.suffix.lower()
    204 +    if ext in IMAGE_EXTS:
    205 +        return "image"
    206 +    if ext in VIDEO_EXTS:
    207 +        return "video"
    208 +    if ext in TEXT_EXTS:
    209 +        return "text"
    210 +    return "other"
    211 +
    212 +
    213 +def analyze_image(api_key, model, path):
    214 +    mime_type = mimetypes.guess_type(path.name)[0] or "image/png"
    215 +    inline = base64.b64encode(path.read_bytes()).decode("ascii")
    216 +    parts = [
    217 +        {"text": generation_prompt(path.name, "image")},
    218 +        {"inline_data": {"mime_type": mime_type, "data": inline}},
    219 +    ]
    220 +    result = generate_content(api_key, model, parts)
    221 +    result["analysis_mode"] = "inline_image"
    222 +    return result
    223 +
    224 +
    225 +def analyze_text(api_key, model, path, max_text_chars):
    226 +    excerpt = read_text_excerpt(path, max_text_chars)
    227 +    parts = [
    228 +        {"text": generation_prompt(path.name, "document")},
    229 +        {"text": excerpt},
    230 +    ]
    231 +    result = generate_content(api_key, model, parts)
    232 +    result["analysis_mode"] = "text_input"
    233 +    result["excerpt_chars"] = len(excerpt)
    234 +    return result
    235 +
    236 +
    237 +def analyze_video(api_key, model, path):
    238 +    mime_type = mimetypes.guess_type(path.name)[0] or "video/mp4"
    239 +    uploaded = upload_file(api_key, path, mime_type)
    240 +    uploaded = wait_until_active(api_key, uploaded)
    241 +    try:
    242 +        parts = [
    243 +            {"text": generation_prompt(path.name, "video")},
    244 +            {"file_data": {"mime_type": mime_type, "file_uri": uploaded["uri"]}},
    245 +        ]
    246 +        result = generate_content(api_key, model, parts)
    247 +        result["analysis_mode"] = "uploaded_video"
    248 +        return result
    249 +    finally:
    250 +        if uploaded.get("name"):
    251 +            delete_file(api_key, uploaded["name"])
    252 +
    253 +
    254 +def analyze_other(api_key, model, path):
    255 +    parts = [
    256 +        {
    257 +            "text": (
    258 +                generation_prompt(path.name, "file")
    259 +                + " This file type is not directly supported for binary upload in this to
         ol. "
    260 +                "Infer from the filename and metadata only."
    261 +            )
    262 +        },
    263 +        {
    264 +            "text": json.dumps(
    265 +                {
    266 +                    "file_name": path.name,
    267 +                    "suffix": path.suffix.lower(),
    268 +                    "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size,
    269 +                }
    270 +            )
    271 +        },
    272 +    ]
    273 +    result = generate_content(api_key, model, parts)
    274 +    result["analysis_mode"] = "metadata_only"
    275 +    return result
    276 +
    277 +
    278 +def analyze_path(api_key, model, path, max_text_chars):
    279 +    kind = classify(path)
    280 +    if kind == "image":
    281 +        return kind, analyze_image(api_key, model, path)
    282 +    if kind == "video":
    283 +        return kind, analyze_video(api_key, model, path)
    284 +    if kind == "text":
    285 +        return kind, analyze_text(api_key, model, path, max_text_chars)
    286 +    return kind, analyze_other(api_key, model, path)
    287 +
    288 +
    289 +def render_markdown(report):
    290 +    lines = []
    291 +    lines.append(f"# Recent Downloads First Pass - {report['generatedAt']}")
    292 +    lines.append("")
    293 +    lines.append(f"- Folder: `{report['downloadsDir']}`")
    294 +    lines.append(f"- Count: `{report['count']}`")
    295 +    lines.append(f"- Gemini model: `{report['model']}`")
    296 +    lines.append(f"- API key env: `{report['apiKeyEnv']}`")
    297 +    lines.append("")
    298 +    lines.append("## Batch Themes")
    299 +    lines.append("")
    300 +    for theme in report["batchThemes"]:
    301 +        lines.append(f"- {theme}")
    302 +    lines.append("")
    303 +    for index, item in enumerate(report["items"], start=1):
    304 +        lines.append(f"## {index:02d}. {item['name']}")
    305 +        lines.append("")
    306 +        lines.append(f"- Type: `{item['kind']}`")
    307 +        lines.append(f"- Modified: `{item['lastWriteTime']}`")
    308 +        lines.append(f"- Size bytes: `{item['sizeBytes']}`")
    309 +        if item.get("imageMeta"):
    310 +            lines.append(
    311 +                f"- Dimensions: `{item['imageMeta'].get('width')}x{item['imageMeta'].get(
         'height')}`"
    312 +            )
    313 +        lines.append(f"- Summary: {item['summary']}")
    314 +        if item.get("primarySubjects"):
    315 +            lines.append("- Primary subjects:")
    316 +            for subject in item["primarySubjects"]:
    317 +                lines.append(f"  - {subject}")
    318 +        if item.get("visibleText"):
    319 +            lines.append("- Visible text:")
    320 +            for text in item["visibleText"]:
    321 +                lines.append(f"  - {text}")
    322 +        if item.get("keyIdeas"):
    323 +            lines.append("- Key ideas:")
    324 +            for idea in item["keyIdeas"]:
    325 +                lines.append(f"  - {idea}")
    326 +        if item.get("actionSignals"):
    327 +            lines.append("- Action signals:")
    328 +            for action in item["actionSignals"]:
    329 +                lines.append(f"  - {action}")
    330 +        if item.get("sensitivityFlags"):
    331 +            lines.append("- Sensitivity flags:")
    332 +            for flag in item["sensitivityFlags"]:
    333 +                lines.append(f"  - {flag}")
    334 +        lines.append(f"- Confidence: `{item['confidence']}`")
    335 +        if item.get("error"):
    336 +            lines.append(f"- Error: `{item['error']}`")
    337 +        lines.append("")
    338 +    return "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
    339 +
    340 +
    341 +def collect_batch_themes(items):
    342 +    counts = {}
    343 +    for item in items:
    344 +        for idea in item.get("keyIdeas") or []:
    345 +            key = idea.strip()
    346 +            if not key:
    347 +                continue
    348 +            counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    349 +    ranked = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))
    350 +    return [f"{idea} ({count})" for idea, count in ranked[:12]]
    351 +
    352 +
    353 +def main():
    354 +    args = parse_args()
    355 +    api_key, api_key_env = pick_api_key()
    356 +    downloads_dir = Path(args.downloads_dir).expanduser().resolve()
    357 +    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir).expanduser().resolve()
    358 +    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    359 +
    360 +    files = sorted(
    361 +        [path for path in downloads_dir.iterdir() if path.is_file()],
    362 +        key=lambda path: path.stat().st_mtime,
    363 +        reverse=True,
    364 +    )[: args.count]
    365 +
    366 +    items = []
    367 +    for path in files:
    368 +        record = {
    369 +            "path": str(path),
    370 +            "name": path.name,
    371 +            "sizeBytes": path.stat().st_size,
    372 +            "lastWriteTime": datetime.fromtimestamp(path.stat().st_mtime).astimezone().is
         oformat(timespec="seconds"),
    373 +            "imageMeta": image_metadata(path) if path.suffix.lower() in IMAGE_EXTS else {
         },
    374 +        }
    375 +        try:
    376 +            kind, analysis = analyze_path(api_key, args.model, path, args.max_text_chars)
    377 +            record["kind"] = kind
    378 +            record["analysisMode"] = analysis.get("analysis_mode", "")
    379 +            record["summary"] = analysis.get("summary", "").strip()
    380 +            record["visibleText"] = analysis.get("visible_text", [])
    381 +            record["primarySubjects"] = analysis.get("primary_subjects", [])
    382 +            record["keyIdeas"] = analysis.get("key_ideas", [])
    383 +            record["actionSignals"] = analysis.get("action_signals", [])
    384 +            record["sensitivityFlags"] = analysis.get("sensitivity_flags", [])
    385 +            record["confidence"] = analysis.get("confidence", "low")
    386 +        except Exception as exc:
    387 +            record["kind"] = classify(path)
    388 +            record["summary"] = "Analysis failed."
    389 +            record["visibleText"] = []
    390 +            record["primarySubjects"] = []
    391 +            record["keyIdeas"] = []
    392 +            record["actionSignals"] = []
    393 +            record["sensitivityFlags"] = []
    394 +            record["confidence"] = "low"
    395 +            record["error"] = str(exc)
    396 +        items.append(record)
    397 +
    398 +    report = {
    399 +        "generatedAt": iso_now(),
    400 +        "downloadsDir": str(downloads_dir),
    401 +        "count": len(items),
    402 +        "model": args.model,
    403 +        "apiKeyEnv": api_key_env,
    404 +        "items": items,
    405 +        "batchThemes": collect_batch_themes(items),
    406 +    }
    410 +    md_path = output_dir / f"{args.prefix}-{stamp}.md"
    411 +    json_path.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=True) + "\n", encoding
         ="utf-8")
    412 +    md_path.write_text(render_markdown(report), encoding="utf-8")
    413 +
    414 +    print(json.dumps({"ok": True, "json": str(json_path), "markdown": str(md_path), "coun
         t": len(items)}))
    415 +
    416 +
    417 +if __name__ == "__main__":
    418 +    try:
    419 +        main()
    420 +    except Exception as exc:
    421 +        print(json.dumps({"ok": False, "error": str(exc)}))
    422 +        sys.exit(1)

     11 +const STOPWORDS = new Set([
     12 +  "a", "an", "and", "are", "as", "at", "be", "been", "being", "but", "by", "for", "from",
          "if", "in", "into",
     13 +  "is", "it", "its", "of", "on", "or", "that", "the", "their", "them", "there", "these",

     18 +let cache = {
     19 +  loadedAt: 0,
     20 +  signature: "",
     21 +  catalogRows: [],
     22 +  entries: []

     24 +
     25 +function normalizeIxId(value) {
     26 +  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,4})/);
     27 +  if (!match) return "";
     28 +  return `IX-${String(match[1]).padStart(3, "0")}`;
     29 +}
     30 +
     31 +function normalizeArray(value) {
     32 +  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
     33 +    return value.map((item) => String(item || "").trim()).filter(Boolean);
     34 +  }
     35 +  const text = String(value || "").trim();
     36 +  if (!text) return [];
     37 +  const cleaned = text.startsWith("[") && text.endsWith("]")
     38 +    ? text.slice(1, -1)
     39 +    : text;
     40 +  return cleaned
     41 +    .split(",")
     42 +    .map((item) => String(item || "").trim())
     43 +    .filter(Boolean)
     44 +    .map((item) => item.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, ""));
     45 +}
     46 +
     47 +function cleanLine(text) {
     48 +  return String(text || "")
     49 +    .replace(/\r/g, "")
     50 +    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
     51 +    .trim();
     52 +}
     53 +
     54 +function parseFrontMatter(rawText) {
     55 +  const source = String(rawText || "");
     56 +  if (!source.startsWith("---")) {
     57 +    return { attrs: {}, body: source };
     58 +  }
     59 +  const marker = source.indexOf("\n---", 3);
     60 +  if (marker < 0) {
     61 +    return { attrs: {}, body: source };
     62 +  }
     63 +  const frontmatter = source.slice(3, marker).trim();
     64 +  const body = source.slice(marker + 4).trim();
     65 +  const attrs = {};
     66 +  for (const line of frontmatter.split(/\r?\n/g)) {
     67 +    const match = line.match(/^([a-zA-Z0-9_]+):\s*(.+)$/);
     68 +    if (!match) continue;
     69 +    const key = String(match[1] || "").trim();
     70 +    const value = String(match[2] || "").trim();
     71 +    if (!key) continue;
     72 +    if (["tags", "chain", "agents"].includes(key)) {
     73 +      attrs[key] = normalizeArray(value);
     74 +    } else {
     75 +      attrs[key] = value.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
     76 +    }
     77 +  }
     78 +  return { attrs, body };
     79 +}
     80 +
     81 +function parseCatalogRows(rawText) {
     82 +  const rows = [];
     83 +  for (const line of String(rawText || "").split(/\r?\n/g)) {
     84 +    const match = line.match(/^\|\s*(\d{1,4})\s*\|/);
     85 +    if (!match) continue;
     86 +    const parts = line.split("|").map((part) => part.trim());
     87 +    if (parts.length < 8) continue;
     88 +    rows.push({
     89 +      ix: normalizeIxId(match[1]),
     90 +      name: String(parts[2] || "").trim(),
     91 +      type: String(parts[3] || "").trim(),
     92 +      tags: normalizeArray(parts[4]),
     93 +      chain: normalizeArray(String(parts[5] || "").replace(/\u2192/g, ",")),
     94 +      agents: normalizeArray(parts[6])
     95 +    });
     96 +  }
     97 +  return rows;
     98 +}
     99 +
    100 +function buildSummaryFromBody(body) {
    101 +  const lines = String(body || "")
    102 +    .split(/\r?\n/g)
    103 +    .map((line) => cleanLine(line))
    104 +    .filter(Boolean)
    105 +    .filter((line) => !/^#/.test(line));
    106 +  if (lines.length < 1) {
    107 +    return "";
    108 +  }
    109 +  return cleanLine(lines.slice(0, 3).join(" "));
    110 +}
    111 +
    112 +function buildSnippet(text, terms, maxChars = 220) {
    113 +  const source = cleanLine(text);
    114 +  if (!source) return "";
    115 +  const lower = source.toLowerCase();
    116 +  let firstIndex = -1;
    117 +  for (const term of terms) {
    118 +    const idx = lower.indexOf(term);
    119 +    if (idx >= 0 && (firstIndex < 0 || idx < firstIndex)) {
    120 +      firstIndex = idx;
    121 +    }
    122 +  }
    123 +  if (firstIndex < 0) {
    124 +    if (source.length <= maxChars) return source;
    125 +    return `${source.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxChars - 3)).trim()}...`;
    126 +  }
    127 +  const start = Math.max(0, firstIndex - 80);
    128 +  const end = Math.min(source.length, firstIndex + maxChars - 40);
    129 +  const snippet = source.slice(start, end).trim();
    130 +  return snippet.length <= maxChars
    131 +    ? snippet
    132 +    : `${snippet.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxChars - 3)).trim()}...`;
    133 +}
    134 +
    135 +function tokenizeQuery(text) {
    136 +  return Array.from(
    137 +    new Set(
    138 +      String(text || "")
    139 +        .toLowerCase()
    140 +        .split(/[^a-z0-9]+/g)
    141 +        .map((token) => token.trim())
    142 +        .filter((token) => token.length >= 2 && token.length <= 40 && !STOPWORDS.has(toke
         n))
    143 +    )
    144 +  ).slice(0, 20);
    145 +}
    146 +
    147 +function extractIxRefs(text) {
    148 +  const refs = new Set();
    149 +  const source = String(text || "");
    150 +  const regex = /\bix[-_\s]?(\d{1,4})\b/gi;
    151 +  let match = regex.exec(source);
    152 +  while (match) {
    153 +    refs.add(normalizeIxId(match[1]));
    154 +    match = regex.exec(source);
    155 +  }
    156 +  return Array.from(refs);
    157 +}
    158 +
    159 +function computeSignature(catalogStat, ixFiles) {
    160 +  const payload = [
    161 +    `${catalogPath}:${Number(catalogStat?.mtimeMs || 0)}:${Number(catalogStat?.size || 0)
         }`,
    162 +    ...ixFiles.map((row) => `${row.path}:${row.mtimeMs}:${row.size}`)
    163 +  ];
    164 +  return payload.join("|");
    165 +}
    166 +
    167 +function listIxFiles() {
    168 +  if (!fs.existsSync(agentIndexRoot)) {
    169 +    return [];
    170 +  }
    171 +  return fs.readdirSync(agentIndexRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    172 +    .filter((entry) => entry.isFile() && /^IX-\d{3,4}\.md$/i.test(entry.name))
    173 +    .map((entry) => {
    174 +      const fullPath = path.join(agentIndexRoot, entry.name);
    175 +      const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
    176 +      return {
    177 +        path: fullPath,
    178 +        mtimeMs: Number(stat.mtimeMs || 0),
    179 +        size: Number(stat.size || 0)
    180 +      };
    181 +    })
    182 +    .sort((a, b) => a.path.localeCompare(b.path));
    183 +}
    184 +
    185 +function loadAgentIndex(options = {}) {
    186 +  const force = Boolean(options.force);
    187 +  const ttlMs = Math.max(1000, Number(options.cacheTtlMs) || DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL_MS);
    188 +  if (!fs.existsSync(catalogPath)) {
    189 +    return {
    190 +      loadedAt: 0,
    191 +      catalogRows: [],
    192 +      entries: []
    193 +    };
    194 +  }
    195 +
    196 +  const catalogStat = fs.statSync(catalogPath);
    197 +  const ixFiles = listIxFiles();
    198 +  const signature = computeSignature(catalogStat, ixFiles);
    199 +  const now = Date.now();
    200 +  const cacheAgeMs = now - Number(cache.loadedAt || 0);
    201 +  const canReuse = !force
    202 +    && cache.loadedAt > 0
    203 +    && cache.signature === signature
    204 +    && cacheAgeMs <= ttlMs;
    205 +
    206 +  if (canReuse) {
    207 +    return {
    208 +      loadedAt: cache.loadedAt,
    209 +      catalogRows: cache.catalogRows.slice(),
    210 +      entries: cache.entries.slice()
    211 +    };
    212 +  }
    213 +
    214 +  const catalogRaw = String(fs.readFileSync(catalogPath, "utf8") || "");
    215 +  const catalogRows = parseCatalogRows(catalogRaw);
    216 +  const catalogMap = new Map(catalogRows.map((row) => [row.ix, row]));
    217 +  const entries = [];
    218 +  for (const file of ixFiles) {
    219 +    let rawText = "";
    220 +    try {
    221 +      rawText = String(fs.readFileSync(file.path, "utf8") || "");
    222 +    } catch (_error) {
    223 +      rawText = "";
    224 +    }
    225 +    if (!rawText.trim()) {
    226 +      continue;
    227 +    }
    228 +    const parsed = parseFrontMatter(rawText);
    229 +    const ix = normalizeIxId(parsed.attrs.ix || path.basename(file.path));
    230 +    const catalogRow = catalogMap.get(ix) || {};
    231 +    const body = String(parsed.body || "").trim();
    232 +    const summary = buildSummaryFromBody(body);
    233 +    const title = cleanLine(parsed.attrs.name || catalogRow.name || ix);
    234 +    const type = cleanLine(parsed.attrs.type || catalogRow.type || "reference");
    235 +    const tags = normalizeArray(parsed.attrs.tags || catalogRow.tags || []);
    236 +    const chain = normalizeArray(parsed.attrs.chain || catalogRow.chain || []);
    237 +    const source = path.relative(projectRoot, file.path).replace(/\\/g, "/");
    238 +    const searchable = [
    239 +      ix,
    240 +      title,
    241 +      type,
    242 +      tags.join(" "),
    243 +      chain.join(" "),
    244 +      summary,
    245 +      body
    246 +    ].filter(Boolean).join("\n");
    247 +    entries.push({
    248 +      ix,
    249 +      title,
    250 +      type,
    251 +      tags,
    252 +      chain,
    253 +      source,
    254 +      absolutePath: file.path,
    255 +      updatedAt: new Date(file.mtimeMs).toISOString(),
    256 +      summary,
    257 +      body,
    258 +      searchable,
    259 +      searchableLower: searchable.toLowerCase()
    260 +    });
    261 +  }
    262 +
    263 +  cache = {
    264 +    loadedAt: now,
    265 +    signature,
    266 +    catalogRows,
    267 +    entries
    268 +  };
    269 +  return {
    270 +    loadedAt: cache.loadedAt,
    271 +    catalogRows: cache.catalogRows.slice(),
    272 +    entries: cache.entries.slice()
    273 +  };
    274 +}
    275 +
    276 +function scoreEntry(entry, query, tokens, ixRefs) {
    277 +  const lower = String(entry.searchableLower || "");
    278 +  const titleLower = String(entry.title || "").toLowerCase();
    279 +  const tagLower = entry.tags.map((item) => String(item || "").toLowerCase());
    280 +  const chainLower = entry.chain.map((item) => String(item || "").toLowerCase());
    281 +  let score = 0;
    282 +
    283 +  for (const ixRef of ixRefs) {
    284 +    if (!ixRef) continue;
    285 +    if (entry.ix === ixRef) {
    286 +      score += 240;
    287 +    }
    288 +    if (chainLower.includes(ixRef.toLowerCase())) {
    289 +      score += 30;
    290 +    }
    291 +  }
    292 +
    293 +  for (const token of tokens) {
    294 +    if (!token) continue;
    295 +    if (titleLower.includes(token)) {
    296 +      score += 18;
    297 +    }
    298 +    if (tagLower.some((value) => value.includes(token))) {
    299 +      score += 12;
    300 +    }
    301 +    if (String(entry.type || "").toLowerCase() === token) {
    302 +      score += 10;
    303 +    }
    304 +    if (chainLower.some((value) => value.includes(token))) {
    305 +      score += 8;
    306 +    }
    307 +    if (lower.includes(token)) {
    308 +      score += 4;
    309 +    }
    310 +  }
    311 +
    312 +  if (query && lower.includes(query.toLowerCase())) {
    313 +    score += 24;
    314 +  }
    315 +
    316 +  return score;
    317 +}
    318 +
    319 +function getAgentIndexStatus(options = {}) {
    320 +  const loaded = loadAgentIndex(options);
    321 +  return {
    322 +    ok: true,
    323 +    enabled: fs.existsSync(catalogPath),
    324 +    loadedAt: loaded.loadedAt ? new Date(loaded.loadedAt).toISOString() : "",
    325 +    root: agentIndexRoot,
    326 +    catalogPath,
    327 +    catalogEntries: loaded.catalogRows.length,
    328 +    ixFiles: loaded.entries.length
    329 +  };
    330 +}
    331 +
    332 +function searchAgentIndex(query, options = {}) {
    333 +  const text = cleanLine(query);
    334 +  const loaded = loadAgentIndex(options);
    335 +  if (!text) {
    336 +    return {
    337 +      ok: true,
    338 +      enabled: fs.existsSync(catalogPath),
    339 +      query: "",
    340 +      count: 0,
    341 +      matches: []
    342 +    };
    343 +  }
    344 +  const tokens = tokenizeQuery(text);
    345 +  const ixRefs = extractIxRefs(text);
    346 +  const safeLimit = Math.max(1, Math.min(30, Number(options.limit) || 6));
    347 +  const scored = loaded.entries
    348 +    .map((entry) => ({
    349 +      entry,
    350 +      score: scoreEntry(entry, text, tokens, ixRefs)
    351 +    }))
    352 +    .filter((row) => row.score > 0)
    353 +    .sort((left, right) => {
    354 +      if (left.score !== right.score) return right.score - left.score;
    355 +      return String(right.entry.updatedAt || "").localeCompare(String(left.entry.updatedA
         t || ""));
    356 +    })
    357 +    .slice(0, safeLimit)
    358 +    .map((row) => ({
    359 +      kind: "index",
    360 +      ix: row.entry.ix,
    361 +      source: row.entry.source,
    362 +      title: row.entry.title,
    363 +      type: row.entry.type,
    364 +      tags: row.entry.tags.slice(0, 12),
    365 +      chain: row.entry.chain.slice(0, 8),
    366 +      line: 1,
    367 +      score: row.score,
    368 +      snippet: buildSnippet(
    369 +        [row.entry.summary, row.entry.body].filter(Boolean).join(" "),
    370 +        tokens.concat(ixRefs.map((value) => value.toLowerCase())),
    371 +        Math.max(80, Math.min(420, Number(options.maxSnippetChars) || 220))
    372 +      ),
    373 +      updatedAt: row.entry.updatedAt
    374 +    }));
    375 +
    376 +  return {
    377 +    ok: true,
    378 +    enabled: fs.existsSync(catalogPath),
    379 +    query: text,
    380 +    tokens,
    381 +    ixRefs,
    382 +    count: scored.length,
    383 +    matches: scored
    384 +  };
    385 +}
    386 +
    387 +function buildAgentIndexContextForPrompt(query, options = {}) {
    388 +  const costMode = String(options.costMode || "low").toLowerCase();
    389 +  const limit = costMode === "quality" ? 4 : costMode === "balanced" ? 3 : 2;
    390 +  const result = searchAgentIndex(query, {
    391 +    limit,
    392 +    maxSnippetChars: costMode === "quality" ? 260 : costMode === "balanced" ? 220 : 180
    393 +  });
    394 +  if (result.count < 1) {
    395 +    return "";
    396 +  }
    397 +  const lines = result.matches.map((row) => {
    398 +    const tagText = row.tags.length > 0 ? ` [${row.tags.join(", ")}]` : "";
    399 +    return `- ${row.ix} ${row.type} "${row.title}"${tagText} ${row.snippet}`;
    400 +  });
    401 +  return ["Agent index:", ...lines].join("\n");
    402 +}
    403 +
    404 +function collectAgentIndexDocuments(limit = 120, options = {}) {
    405 +  const safeLimit = Math.max(1, Math.min(800, Number(limit) || 120));
    406 +  const loaded = loadAgentIndex(options);
    407 +  return loaded.entries.slice(0, safeLimit).map((entry) => ({
    408 +    id: `agent-index:${entry.ix}`,
    409 +    sourceKind: "agent_index",
    410 +    sourceLabel: entry.source,
    411 +    title: `${entry.ix} ${entry.title}`.trim(),
    412 +    updatedAt: entry.updatedAt,
    413 +    text: [
    414 +      entry.ix,
    415 +      `Name: ${entry.title}`,

    13 +function get(path) {
    14 +  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    15 +    const req = http.get(`${BASE}${path}`, { timeout: 15000 }, (res) => {
    16 +      let body = "";
    17 +      res.on("data", (chunk) => { body += chunk; });
    18 +      res.on("end", () => {
    19 +        try {
    20 +          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, body: JSON.parse(body) });
    21 +        } catch (_error) {
    22 +          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, body });
    23 +        }
    24 +      });
    25 +    });
    26 +    req.on("error", reject);
    27 +    req.on("timeout", () => {
    28 +      req.destroy();
    29 +      reject(new Error("timeout"));
    30 +    });
    31 +  });
    32 +}
